# TRUTH-MAP — who owns each fact, how the spine + Arc connect, where the picture fractures

**What this is.** The third companion to the screen docs, and the one that was missing. `SCREEN-INVENTORY.md` says *what each screen is*; `SCREEN-CONNECTIVITY.md` says *what each screen is wired to*. **This doc says, for each FACT the app shows, which layer OWNS it and whether every screen that shows it AGREES** — plus the verified fractures where they don't. It exists so a future session (or Claude) never has to re-trace the app to know where truth lives, and never drifts building a thing the app already does.

**Method.** Code-derived + adversarially verified 2026-07-09/10 (three read-only traces). Where a claim is load-bearing it carries a `file:line`. Update this the same session any authority or fracture changes.

**Root-cause note (why this doc exists):** on 2026-07-09 a session built a whole endurance-interpretation "engine" for a read the app already had (spine decoupling + the carryover RPE gauge), aimed at a screen it never pinned down, while the real fractures (strength self-contradiction, Baselines FTP) sat untouched. That is exactly what this map prevents.

---

## 1. The four truth layers (what each OWNS)

The app is **one shell** switched by state flags, not routes (`SCREEN-INVENTORY.md`). Underneath, truth lives in four layers. Each owns different facts; they are meant to be **layered, not parallel**.

| Layer | What it is | Owns | Written by | Key file |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **user_baselines** | Your reference anchors | FTP, LTHR/max-HR, threshold + easy pace, swim CSS, 1RMs | you (Baselines screen) + `learn-fitness-profile` | `user_baselines.{performance_numbers, learned_fitness, configured_hr_zones}` |
| **THE SPINE** | Per-discipline fitness **verdicts** (trend/direction, not absolute numbers) | run durability + efficiency, bike power + efficiency, swim pace + rest, strength volume + e1RM, and the one rolled-up fitness direction | `compute-snapshot` (current week only) via `assembleStateTrends` | `athlete_snapshot.state_trends_v1` · `_shared/state-trend/assemble.ts` |
| **THE ARC** | The assembler that gathers everything AROUND the spine | goals, plan position, baselines, memory, projections, cycling CTL/ATL/TSB — and a **read-only pass-through of the spine** | `getArcContext` (reads, never writes the spine) | `_shared/arc-context.ts` |
| **THE COACH PAYLOAD** | The State screen's data bundle | LOAD/ACWR + reconciled verdict, BODY/RPE, the week headline, the b2 execution rows, per-lift verdicts | `coach` (reads spine cached + snapshot + response_model) | `weekly_state_v1` · `coach/index.ts` |

**The clean part:** the Arc **reads** the spine (`arc-context.ts:1185-1197`, "no computation, no write") ⟨A31⟩ — it does not mint a competing per-discipline verdict. Fitness direction, load, and RPE are each single-source. So the *core* of the app tells one story.

---

## 2. Which screen reads which layer (pointers; detail in SCREEN-CONNECTIVITY)

| Screen | Component | Reads |
|---|---|---|
| **State** | `context/StateTab.tsx` | Coach payload (`weekly_state_v1`) for LOAD/BODY/headline/b2/per-lift **+ the spine as assembled on the SERVER** (`useStateTrends` renders `weekly_state_v1.trends.display` verbatim — zero client math, no fallback assembly) for the Performance-section trends ⟨A31⟩ **+ Arc** for readiness/longitudinal |
| **Baselines** | `TrainingBaselines.tsx` | `user_baselines` raw (the only screen that does) + Arc for suggestions |
| **Workout · Performance tab** | `UnifiedWorkoutView`→`MobileSummary` | `session_detail_v1` (from `workout-detail`), which reads the spine **cached** |
| **Workout · Details tab** | `CompletedTab` / `StrengthCompletedView` | same `session_detail_v1` contract, read-only |

## 3. Per-fact authority table (the anti-drift tool)

For any fact, this says who owns it and whether the screens agree. **Before building anything about a fact, read its row.**

| Fact | Authority (single source of truth) | Read by | Coherent? |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Fitness direction** (improving/holding/sliding) | SPINE → `rollupFitnessDirection` (`assemble.ts:785`) ⟨A31⟩ | State (cached), coach (cached), workout-detail (cached), analyzers (via Arc) | ✅ one authority, one cache — the client stopped recomputing (`useStateTrends.ts:95-96`) | ⟨A31⟩
| **Load / ACWR / "balanced"** | one algorithm `_shared/acwr.ts:computeAcwr` (D-236); reconciled verdict = `load-status-reconcile.ts` (D-260) | State (from coach) | ✅ single-algorithm (dual-computed: snapshot persists, coach recomputes; equivalence-tested) |
| **RPE / "how it feels"** | one object `response-model/weekly.ts` `endurance.rpe` | State header, BODY row, readiness — all deref the same object | ✅ cannot diverge within a payload |
| **Run durability** (decoupling, Friel band) | SPINE `state_trends_v1.run.decoupling` | State (live), Performance tab (cached, but not currently rendered) | 🔴 **FRACTURED — corrected 2026-07-31 ([D-346]).** One authority, fed by a gate that discriminates nothing: `isSteadyAerobic(workout_type)` reads `heart_rate_summary.workoutType`, which is `steady_state` on **all 25** of this athlete's runs (an 11-minute jog and a hill session included). Hill drills reached the trend at 24.9% and State reported declining fitness. ⛔ **The "heat seam CLOSED (D-275)" claim below was FALSE for 18 days** — D-275 was reversed by D-283 and there is no heat filter. |
| **1RM anchor** (per-lift) | `resolveStrengthCapacity` — **typed wins** (D-231) | coach, materialize, per-lift verdict | ✅ **the model the others should copy** |
| **FTP** | `resolveCurrentFtp` (learned-first, ≥medium conf) | Baselines, Athletic Record, cycling analyzer — ALL through the resolver now | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-07-10/11** (was fracture #2 — see below) |
| **Strength trend** (volume / e1RM) | — | see fracture #1 | 🔴 **FRACTURE** — three engines on one screen |
| **Session drift** (is it steady, and what is the number) | `_shared/session-detail/session-steadiness.ts` decides steady; `drift-pct.ts resolveSessionDrift` gives the number; `vt1-window-drift.ts` windows a long session with sets | Performance tiles, Today's good-news line, State's drift chart, the fact-packet flags — all by import | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-09-12.** Was five answers: the boom line called the shared rule with no materials, `compute-snapshot` kept its own steady test plus a fourth fallback (`workout_facts.drift`) no other screen could reach, its second call site passed no interval flag, and `endurance-checkpoint` read `run_facts.hr_drift_pct` raw and printed it as "decoupling". Verified on a throwaway account, eight sessions, three seeds: Performance and State agree number for number. |
| **The plan line** ("Standard Focus · week 2 of 12") | `_shared/plan-line.ts` — one composer; `workout-detail` stamps `block.line`, `coach` stamps it on the State card, `get-week` stamps `weekPosition` | Performance lift header + run/ride tiles + swim card, State strength row, Today's date header | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-09-12.** Was four composers in three grammars, including a `Build` phase word defaulted before any evidence. The screens print; only Today adds the date, which it owns. |
| **Is this session indoors, and what is it called** | `_shared/indoor-session.ts` (the predicate, since 2026-09-09) + `src/lib/session-display-name.ts` (the words, 2026-09-12) | Today's cards, Performance header, the map placeholder, the imported-row name, CompletedTab's placeholder gate | ✅ **CLOSED 2026-09-12.** Was six naming ladders and three separate indoor tests; none of them ever called a RIDE indoors, so a trainer ride said "Ride" on every screen. |
| **Per-session execution** (exec % / analysis) | `session_detail_v1.execution` (`build.ts:837`) ⟨A31⟩ | Workout Performance/Details tabs | ✅ single-source (workout-only) |
| **Bike "how's the bike"** | split: spine `bike.power` trend vs Arc `cycling_fitness` {ctl,atl,tsb} | State / narrative | ⚠️ two adjacent reads, unreconciled (fracture #7) |

---

**✅/⚠️ #8 — HEART-RATE ANCHORS AND ZONES: PER SPORT (2026-08-20). Read the wording, it is deliberate.**

- **Threshold HR — CLOSED.** `resolveCurrentLthr` now takes a `sport` option (the pattern
  `resolve-current-max-hr` already set). The bike previously had **no owner** and three private chains
  (`compute-workout-analysis`, `calculate-workload`, `_shared/ride-easy-hr.ts`), none with the D-284
  sample-count gate. All three route through the resolver.
- **HR ZONES — CLOSED, and this was the bigger one.** One `zones` array, built from
  `runLTHR || rideLTHR` (run PREFERRED), was **priority 1 in `compute-workout-analysis:1580` for every
  discipline**, above every resolver. Rides were binned against RUNNING zones — cycling HR sits 5-10
  bpm under running at the same effort, so each ride landed a zone easy and the time-in-zone the 80/20
  read rests on was wrong for the bike. `TrainingBaselines` now writes `zones_run` / `zones_ride`; the
  shared `zones` stays as the fallback because **Strava genuinely has one set per athlete**.
- ⚠️ **`configured_hr_zones.threshold_heart_rate` — CLOSED FOR READERS, OPEN AS A DATA MODEL.**
  Nothing bike-related reads it. But `TrainingBaselines.tsx` **still writes it** — now only when one
  sport has an anchor, so it cannot be the wrong sport's, yet the field is still there and still filled
  from the run number in the single-sport case. ⛔ **Do not shorten this row to "closed."** The next
  session reads "closed" and stops looking. The real fix is the WRITER emitting per-sport fields only,
  not a guard on a reader.
- **The bike's threshold was a formula, and now is not.** Verified on the real account: 20 rides,
  high-confidence max HR, **zero** threshold candidates, and `90% of observed max (estimated)`
  published at `sample_count: 0`. The filter required a WHOLE RIDE to average 85-95% of max — real
  riding never does. `power_curve` now carries the HR *during* each best window, so the bike's
  threshold and its FTP describe one effort. ⚠️ No backfill; needs new rides.

**✅ #9 — ANCHOR READS ARE ENFORCED, NOT JUST DOCUMENTED (2026-08-20).** §5's rule ("read through its
resolver — never the raw column") had nothing behind it, so every new surface grew its own chain.
`_shared/anchor-resolver-lint.test.ts` froze 49 raw readers and the ledger MAY ONLY SHRINK; it is at
**32 — 26 legitimate, 6 swim (unowned by design)**. The spine, the Arc's coach text, race readiness,
the marathon builder and the goal builder were all routed. ⛔ The spine (`compute-snapshot`) had read
the learned threshold with **no confidence check at all** and fell back to a `performance_numbers`
spelling nothing writes; an unrelated 8-run floor is the only reason a contaminated 2-run read never
printed a race time on State.

## 4. The verified fractures (recorded so they're never rediscovered)

**Per-discipline cohesion verdict (traced + verified 2026-07-10):**
- ⛔ **RUN — NOT CLEAN. CORRECTED 2026-07-31, and this line is why the row was rebuilt fifteen times.**
  > **What this entry used to say:** *"RUN — CLEAN. One rendered authority; the model the others should copy. Heat seam closed by D-275 — heat-confounded runs are excluded from the substrate."*
  >
  > **Both halves were false.** (a) D-275 was **reversed by D-283**; there is no heat filter in the code, and this line kept presenting the dead decision as live. (b) The single authority is real but its **gate is a constant** — `isSteadyAerobic(workout_type)` reads a field that says `steady_state` on every run ever logged, so nothing is excluded and a hill session in 30°C heat lands on the durability trend as a clean steady measurement.
  >
  > ⛔ **THE COST OF THIS ONE LINE.** Fifteen decision entries touch this row (D-036 … D-345). A fresh session opens TRUTH-MAP, reads "RUN — CLEAN, the model the others should copy", concludes the symptom in front of it must come from somewhere new, and builds something new. Three separate sessions in three days wrote a run's intent into three different fields, none of which the gate reads. **A doc that says "clean" about a broken thing does not merely fail to help — it actively routes every future session away from the fault.**
- **RUN — the real state (2026-07-31).** One authority, starved gate, and a substrate that is under-filled: on 164 `route_progress_metrics` rows, `temp_f` is present on 115, `decoupling_pct` on 83, and `effort_adjusted_pace_sec_per_km` — the one column that table owns — on **8**. The heat-de-confounded same-route engine (`_shared/heat-adjust.ts`) is complete, tested, and wired to BOTH surfaces: the per-workout screen (`routeHeadline` via `session-detail/build.ts:17`) and the spine (`routeTrend` via `state-trend/assemble.ts:24`, called at `:345`), which is what the State run efficiency row renders. ⟨A31⟩
- **STRENGTH — CONTRADICTING (worst).** Three visible engines; the e1RM fact is computed from two different data trails (fracture #1).
- **BIKE — MIXED.** Fitness *direction* is clean — one rendered authority; the CTL/ATL/TSB "form" second engine (Arc `cycling_fitness`) is **internal-only, never rendered** (and there's even a *third* CTL/ATL/TSB in `analyze-cycling-workout.fitness_v1`, prose-only). But **efficiency has two visible engines** on State — spine 56-day HR-at-power vs coach 7-day HR-drift — only saved from a naked clash by the scope labels ("last 7 days" vs "trends over recent weeks"). (The **FTP fracture #2 is now CLOSED** — all reads route through `resolveCurrentFtp`, fixed 2026-07-10/11; see below.)
- **SWIM — BROKEN, not contradicting.** No two-engines-one-fact clash (rendered pace reads are single-sourced, D-182). The problems are: a single **provisional/`needs_data`** engine, **no swim-native display template** (falls through the endurance/run layout — Q-038 Layer 2, still open; the June duration-unit "2263% adherence" bug is FIXED), and the **CSS anchor is orphaned** — shown on Baselines but read by *nothing* in the swim session verdict, and even its plan-gen use is staged off (`planning-context.ts:238 SWIM_CSS_LIVE = false` ⟨A31⟩). More disconnected than FTP.


**🔴 #1 — Strength contradicts itself on the State screen (LIVE, worst).** Three engines, three windows, one screen:
- b2 7-day execution row ← coach `weekly_state_v1.strength_session_types_7d` (`useCoachWeekContext.ts:266`) ⟨A31⟩
- volume / e1RM trend ← **server-assembled** `state_trends_v1.display.strengthFitness` from `workout_facts.strength_facts` + `exercise_log.estimated_1rm`, rendered verbatim (`StatePerformanceSection.tsx:851`) ⟨A31⟩
- per-lift verdict ← coach `response_model.strength.per_lift` (`StateTab.tsx:1225`) ⟨A31⟩

Nothing forces them to agree → "e1RM improving" can sit above a lift verdict that says decline. **Fix = converge on one strength authority** (the D-231 `resolveStrengthCapacity` pattern is the template).

**✅ #2 — FTP: CLOSED 2026-07-10/11 (was: same anchor, three answers).** All FTP reads now route through the single `resolveCurrentFtp` resolver (learned-first at ≥medium confidence, else manual, else learned-low fallback). Fixed in a prior session (commits `d278cadd` cycling analyzer · `eae2d9aa` Baselines · `00dbc9f2` Plans-tab watts; Athletic Record already used it). Verified by code trace 2026-07-11 (reconciling prior-session work — this map was written before the fix and lagged). The former fracture:
- ~~Baselines showed manual-first~~ → now `resolveCurrentFtp` (`eae2d9aa`).
- Athletic Record showed learned-first via `resolveCurrentFtp` (was already correct).
- ~~`analyze-cycling-workout` read `performance_numbers.ftp` only, ignoring learned~~ → now routes through the resolver (`d278cadd`), so the power band the spine efficiency trend is built from matches what the screens show, and a learned-only rider gets a real band (no more null → no verdict).

**Still open (bike, separate):** efficiency has two *visible* engines on State (spine 56-day HR-at-power vs coach 7-day HR-drift) — contained by scope labels, lower priority. CTL/ATL/TSB triplication is latent (internal-only, never rendered). 1RMs use the same resolver pattern via `resolveStrengthCapacity`.

**⚠️ #3 — Metric easy-pace unit mislabel (latent).** Baselines hardcodes `/mi` (`TrainingBaselines.tsx:1314`; the formatters at `:510` and `:518` hardcode it too) ⟨A31⟩; AppContext stores `/km` for metric users (`AppContext.tsx:359`). Masked today only because the run analyzer is suffix-blind.

**✅ #4 — Live-vs-cached freshness fork: CLOSED.** There is no live client recompute any more — `useStateTrends` is a pure renderer of the server-assembled `state_trends_v1` (`useStateTrends.ts:1-6, 95-96`), so State and the Performance tab read the same cached contract by construction. The Performance-tab trend line is also not rendered (`MobileSummary.tsx:163`). ⟨A31⟩

**⚠️ #5–7 — drift risks (not visible contradictions):** client re-implements `FitnessVerdictDivergence` (D-212 mirror, `useCoachWeekContext.ts:70`); `arc-context.ts:369-392` (`projectionDirectionFromDelta`) copies race-readiness projection bands ⟨A31⟩; spine bike-trend vs Arc `cycling_fitness` unreconciled.

## 5. "Where does X belong" (so nobody drifts again)

- **A single session's quality / execution** → `session_detail_v1` (`build.ts`), rendered on the **workout Performance tab**. Planned = execution %, unplanned = analysis. This is the per-session home.
- **A multi-week trend / fitness direction** → the **spine** (`assembleStateTrends`); every surface reads it, don't compute a parallel one.
- **A reference anchor** (FTP, threshold, 1RM, CSS) → `user_baselines`, read through its resolver (`resolveCurrentFtp`, `resolveStrengthCapacity`) — never read the raw column past the resolver.
- **A weekly verdict for the State screen** → the **coach** payload (`weekly_state_v1`); the client renders it, never re-derives it (Law 4).
- **The endurance per-session read on RPE + decoupling already exists** — spine `run.decoupling` + the carryover RPE-vs-typical gauge (`cross-domain-carryover.ts`). Extend those, don't rebuild them.

> **Read-only audit, code as of commit `5137b3ee`+.** Traced from the rendered JSX back to the computing line by three read-only passes (Performance, Details, State); nothing was changed. `sf/` = `supabase/functions/`, `c/` = `src/components/`. **Source** = the field/book citation in the code or `STATE-SOURCES.md`; `OURS` = marked ours; `NONE` = searched (comments beside the constant + STATE-SOURCES) and found neither. **Flags:** `PHONE` = the phone changes the value (not plain formatting) · `TWO` = computed in two or more places that can disagree · `NO-SRC` = a threshold/constant/formula with no citation and no OURS marker. ### Flags, grouped (the reading order for fixing)

**Computed in two places that already disagree or can:** execution score (analyzers vs a drift score of the same name in the summary step); avg pace and moving/distance totals (two server writers race); avg HR and GAP (Details tile vs Performance pill on the same run); ride efficiency factor (Performance vs State); drift (Today skips the easy-portion window; 5% line in four files with a boundary disagreement); easy pace (State row vs Adjust); FTP (snapshot vs resolver); one lift's three numbers (Adjust capacity, e1RM tile, training max); race projection (three producers, bands copied); form rounding (State vs Today); max speed cut (25 vs 30 m/s); RPE (saved to one field, read from another); swim distance and swim pace unit (Details vs Performance); ledger vs code (BODY rows still render; lift chart window 84 d vs 52 wk; rep ceiling 5/8 vs 10).

**Computed on the phone:** every Adjust tab value; State weekly change, bars, readiness arrows, NEXT filter, swim nudge, chart "building" and ranges, race deltas and fallbacks, goal colours; Details zone %, weather, split speed, map overlay pace, axis ticks, fallback derivation when `display_metrics` is missing; Performance duration minutes (rounds vs header floors), swim pace difference, GAP cell fallback to raw pace, route trend line, strides filter, strength duration/lengths from the row.

**No source and no OURS marker:** execution weights and duration tiers; workload "usual" range; heart rate row bands (10%, 3/5 bpm, 75°F); conditions cut-offs; even-pacing 15 s; 25 W pedalling floor; display EMAs 0.18/0.25; max pace/speed filters; HR zone fallbacks (×1.05/×1.15, %HRmax, 180, 220, 0.95); split grade/HR method; State accent thresholds, share rounding, readiness windows, swim nudge numbers, "limited data", "building" <11 wk, strength PR/best slack, week-card 10%, calibration steps (archived program only); race model (VDOT tables, durability, confidence, assessment bands, signal cuts, goal probability formula); next-session 14 days; retest days; test "updated" rule. OURS in code but no ledger row: checkpoint rules, all-out/pull-up 40 sessions, muscle floor 3 sets.



## 7. Every number on the rest of the app (Stage 0 audit, 2026-09-15)

> **Read-only audit, code as of commit `9b1c9171`.** Stage 0 of `docs/WORKORDER-app-one-truth-2026-09-15.md`: the same method as §6 for every screen §6 did not cover — Home / Today · the Week calendar · the strength logger (incl. finish sheet) · plan setup and the plan builder · the plan screens · Adjust and Training Baselines · My Record · Focus / Goals · the post-workout popup · session drawers · notifications and checkpoints · shared and exported numbers. Seven read-only passes, one per screen group; each section names the files it opened and the greps behind every absence claim. Same vocabulary as §6 plus **`LEDGER≠CODE`** = `STATE-SOURCES.md` says one thing, the code does another. Eight load-bearing rows were re-read from the code by the assembling session (marked ✔ in §7.9). Line numbers drift; re-grep before relying on one. Nothing was changed. **Stage 1 re-opens every flagged `file:line` below before any of it reaches a build stage.**

### 7.0 Corrections to §6 found on the way
- §6 cites `c/context/LoadBar.tsx:130-184`; the file is `c/LoadBar.tsx` (no `context/` copy exists — `ls src/components/context/LoadBar.tsx` fails).
- §6's "Today prints planned when actual missing (`c/TodaysEffort.tsx:1247`)" points into `formatRichWorkoutDisplay`, a branch no completed row reaches (`c/SessionDeck.tsx:638` routes every completed row to the session card; the only call is `c/TodaysEffort.tsx:2222` inside the unreachable branch). The TWO flag on the Workload tile should be re-examined in Stage 1.

### 7.1 Home / Today and the Week calendar
> Traced 2026-09-15 (repo commit `9b1c9171`) from `c/TodaysEffort.tsx`, `c/TodayWeather.tsx`, `c/LoadBar.tsx`, `c/HomeTabs.tsx`, `c/WorkoutCalendar.tsx`, `c/WeekGrid.tsx`, `c/RescheduleValidationPopup.tsx`, `c/RescheduleDatePicker.tsx`, `c/SessionDeck.tsx`, `c/CardDeck.tsx`, `c/PlannedSessionHeader.tsx`, `c/PlannedWorkoutSummary.tsx`, `lib/today-lines.ts`, `lib/week-exec-totals.ts`, `lib/load-headline.ts`, `lib/week-change-line.ts`, `lib/fetchWeekUnified.ts`, `lib/sessionWeather.ts`, `lib/build-arc-line.ts`, `lib/utils.ts`, `lib/associate-candidates.ts`, `lib/analysis-state.ts`, `src/utils/strengthFormatter.ts`, `src/hooks/useWeather.ts`; server `sf/get-week/index.ts`, `sf/get-week/week-totals.ts`, `sf/get-week/week-label.ts`, `sf/get-week/planned-duration-label.ts`, `sf/_shared/plan-line.ts`, `sf/_shared/planned-duration.ts`, `sf/_shared/strength-session-minutes.ts`, `sf/_shared/fitness-fatigue.ts`, `sf/_shared/session-boom/{line,compute}.ts`, `sf/_shared/standing-plan/family-lines.ts`, `sf/_shared/swim/swim-plan-summary.ts`, `sf/_shared/session-swap/swap.ts`, `sf/get-weather/index.ts`, `sf/_shared/heat-adjust.ts`, `sf/validate-reschedule/index.ts`, `sf/materialize-plan/index.ts` (pace range, weight_display), `sf/_shared/mobility-sets.ts`.

(c) `grep -n "pace_range|±2|0.02|0.06|2 percent|6 percent" docs/STATE-SOURCES.md` → row 97 only (course-strategy ±2.5%, a different number); (d) `grep -n "day_order|Eddens" docs/STATE-SOURCES.md` → row 142; (e) `grep -n "dailyWorkload|weekWorkload|severity|threshold|suggestion|coachOptions|riskLevel|OURS|Friel|Viada|p[0-9][0-9]" sf/validate-reschedule/index.ts` → no OURS, Friel, Viada or page hit; (f) `grep -rln "WeekGrid" src` → the only renderer is `c/NonRaceBuilder.tsx` (intake), not Home/Week; (h) `ls src/components/context/LoadBar.tsx` → no such file (TRUTH-MAP §6 cites `c/context/LoadBar.tsx`; the file is `c/LoadBar.tsx`); (i) `grep -n "function requiredAdjacencyHours" sf/_shared/session-swap/*.ts` → no definition in that directory (the swap warning's hours are "(inferred)"); (j) `grep -rn "warnings" sf/swap-session sf/_shared/session-swap` → `swap.ts:489-510` is the only writer of warning text.
> Files with no athlete-facing number: `c/HomeTabs.tsx` (two tab labels); `c/RescheduleDatePicker.tsx` (a date, built as local `YYYY-MM-DD` on the phone, `:25-31`); `c/CardDeck.tsx` (gesture constants only — commit 30 px, flick 0.5 px/ms, depth 3 — and the swiped `SessionDeck` "renders nowhere on Today now", `c/SessionDeck.tsx:679-680`); `lib/load-headline.ts` and `lib/week-change-line.ts` (imported by State, not by any Home/Week file — `grep -rn "load-headline\|week-change-line" src/components/TodaysEffort.tsx src/components/WorkoutCalendar.tsx` → none); `lib/week-exec-totals.ts` (the calendar's import was removed, `c/WorkoutCalendar.tsx:27-30`).

### Flags, grouped

**TWO**
- `sf/get-week/index.ts:1473-1484` — "N wks till race": `ceil(days/7)` when a race date exists, else `durationWeeks − currentWeek + 1`; the arc line rounds `days/7` on the phone (`lib/build-arc-line.ts:58`).
- `sf/get-week/index.ts:1601-1636` vs `sf/get-week/week-totals.ts:52-54` — Today's "run 11.2 mi · ride 40.1 mi" and Week's "Done … 24 mi" are two different sums of one week (fresh `workouts` query by status and type run/walk/ride/bike vs `is_executed` items, all sports).
- `lib/utils.ts:63-71` vs `sf/get-week/week-totals.ts:54` — a session's distance on the done card and the Week row comes off a phone ladder (`distance_km` → `distance_m` → bare `distance` assumed km → `overall.distance_m`); the bar and Today's week line read `computed.overall.distance_m` only.
- `c/WorkoutCalendar.tsx:60-68` and `c/TodaysEffort.tsx:293-300` — Monday-anchored week cut in two phone files; the coach cuts the plan week on the plan's own start day (`sf/coach/index.ts:155`).
- `c/TodaysEffort.tsx:2139` and `c/WorkoutCalendar.tsx:1468-1472` — "Rest only when a plan exists", the same rule in two files.
- `lib/associate-candidates.ts:133-144` — one "unlinked" rule, two different inputs (`c/TodaysEffort.tsx:2438-2442` passes `it.planned`; `c/WorkoutCalendar.tsx:982-983` passes status-filtered day rows).
- `sf/validate-reschedule/index.ts:192-221` — a second workload formula (hours × IF² × 100 with IF 1.0/0.75/0.65; strength 80/50) beside `sf/_shared/workload.ts` (§6), printed as "Daily 45 → 130".
- `c/TodaysEffort.tsx:1108-1111` — a second mobility set-count parser (`(\d+)x`, else 1) beside `sf/_shared/mobility-sets.ts:12` (dead branch).
- `c/WorkoutCalendar.tsx:207-239` — a second "easy vs hard by `band:` tag" reading beside the server's session vocabulary (dead label code).

**PHONE**

**Flags, grouped**

TWO
- `sf/save-baseline-test/index.ts:58-62` floors e1RM to 5; `sf/compute-facts/strength-facts-lib.ts:206` + `lib/estimate-1rm.ts:170-174` round to nearest 5 with an RIR fold — the logger's "Saved: N lb" and the test-result card's "e1RM N lb" are two numbers for one set.
- `sf/save-baseline-test/pick.ts:68-111` (heaviest completed working set, AMRAP on tie) vs `sf/analyze-strength-workout/index.ts:776-781` (flagged set, else last working, else heaviest) — two rules for which set is the test.
- `sf/analyze-strength-workout/index.ts:806-813` recomputes updated/kept and ignores the athlete's Keep taken at `sf/save-baseline-test/index.ts:182-185`.
- `sf/materialize-plan/index.ts:1192` floors a rescaled `set_plan` to 5 while `sf/_shared/standing-plan/progression.ts:125` and `lib/exercise-config.ts:3683` round to nearest.
- `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:290-306` total-volume alert vs server volume (`_shared/strength/session-volume.ts`, §6 row).
- `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:2444-2475` prior-session weight written into `set.weight` vs `sf/materialize-plan/index.ts:2762-2765` `weight_suggested` ghost — two sources for the grey starting weight on a by-feel row.
- `lib/rir-format.ts:13` "—" vs `sf/_shared/strength/strength-display-lines.ts:21` "1 to 2" for a row with no reserve target.
- `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:6506` vs `:6849` — two "Saved!" sentences.

PHONE
- `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:1486,3919-3920`, `lib/strength-session-clock.ts:174-209` — elapsed and saved minutes (floor 1, round).
- `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:3688-3722` — anchor steps 1.10A/1.15A computed on the phone (`pretestStepWeights` bundled).
- `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:2369-2475` — prior-session fallback chain (10 workouts, then 400 `exercise_log` rows) decides the grey weight.
- `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:3820-3832`, `lib/rir-format.ts:29-32` — target RIR rounded into a stored value on Done.
- `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:1009-1012,1026-1028` — rep floor and RIR clamp.

### 7.3 Plan setup and the plan builder
> Traced 2026-09-15 (repo at `9b1c9171`) from `c/NonRaceBuilder.tsx` (all 7,397 lines, comments stripped to code), `c/ArcSetupWizard.tsx`, `c/wizard/KnowYourNumbersStep.tsx`, `c/wizard/StepLayout.tsx`, `c/HardSlotChoices.tsx`, `c/EnduranceWeekCard.tsx`, `c/RunStrengthWeekCard.tsx`, `c/RideStrengthWeekCard.tsx`, `c/WeekGrid.tsx`, `c/GoalsScreen.tsx` (calibration modal :973-1030, :1913-1990; add-race priority :2605-2640), `lib/wizard-steps.ts`, `lib/builder-readout.ts`, `lib/week-budget.ts`, `lib/maintenance-volume-band.ts`, `lib/run-volume-tables.ts`, `lib/run-paces-from-threshold.ts`, `lib/run-threshold-from-easy.ts`, `lib/resolve-current-run-pace.ts`, `lib/resolve-current-ftp.ts`, `lib/lifting-commitment.ts`, `lib/session-frequency-defaults.ts`, `lib/hard-slot-choices.ts`, `lib/preview-week-read.ts`, `lib/standing-plan-week-copy.ts`, `lib/standing-plan-week-bounds.ts`, `lib/schedule-gate.ts`, `lib/week-rules-copy.ts`, `lib/pullup-progression.ts`, `lib/non-race-goal-seeds.ts`, `lib/group-ride-route-snapshot.ts`, `lib/run-pace-calibration.ts`; server `sf/create-goal-and-materialize-plan/{index,race-readout,non-race-routing}.ts`, `sf/generate-strength-plan/index.ts`, `sf/_shared/standing-plan/{intake-readout,setup-readout,setup-copy,frames,volume-bounds,demonstrated-history}.ts`, `sf/_shared/week-one-summary.ts`, `sf/_shared/weeks-until-race.ts`, `sf/_shared/pace-benchmark.ts`, `sf/get-arc-context/{intake-readout,history-readout}.ts`, `sf/planning-context/index.ts`, `sf/save-baselines/index.ts`, `sf/generate-run-plan/effort-score.ts`.
> Routes actually reachable (`lib/wizard-steps.ts:272-382`): race = goal → race → level → intent → days → strength → numbers → confirm; strength = goal → train → program → endurance → [accessory] → numbers → schedule → confirm; season wizard = `ArcSetupWizard` (`getSteps` :2376-2411). Steps `posture`, `commitment`, `length`, `run`, `bike`, `volume`, `swim`, `hardday` and the non-strength `accessory` screen are rendered in the file but pushed by no route (`scheduleSteps` :118-184 only pushes `run`/`bike` for a goal that is neither `get_stronger` nor `marathon`; `GOAL_ORDER` :242 has only those two). Their numbers are listed at the end as dead code, not as live findings.
> `sf/planning-context/index.ts`, `materialize-plan`, `activate-plan`, `ensure-planned-ready`, `generate-combined-plan`, `generate-run-plan`, `generate-triathlon-plan`: nothing they compute is printed back on a setup screen except the sessions and durations in the preview week (`WeekGrid`) and the race-path advisories; `planning-context` is not invoked by any file in this group (grep `planning-context` in `src/` → `src/services` only).
> Searches run for absence claims: `grep -n -i` in `docs/STATE-SOURCES.md` for `1.19`, `0.96`, `TIER_SEEDS|tier seed|20, 30, 40|40 miles`, `MAINTENANCE|two-thirds|Hickson`, `chins|pull-up|pullup`, `8 hours|survival|accessory band`, `HOURS_TIERS|weekly_hours|weeks to race|weeks_to_race`, `session_frequency|swims.*bikes.*runs|session frequency`, `MIN_WEEKS|floor weeks|20 weeks|weeks_at_cap`, `3–4%|3-4%|4%`, `90 minutes|75`, `30 minutes`, `advancedTier|extra easy|history supports`, `needsHours|experience chip|Less experienced|More experienced`, `1.0×BW|1.25×BW|BW`, `48-hour|48 hour|6+ hours|six hours`, `resolveCurrentFtp|ftp_source|FTP resolver`, `resolveCurrentRunThresholdPace|threshold resolver|typed threshold`, `toISOString|Monday`, `600|86400|h:mm`, `5,000|6,000|2,500|1,800`, `1–3%|1-3%`, `route|climb|climbNotice`, `plan_start|start date|week of`, `demonstrated|28 days`, `hard_days_priceable`, `test week`, `long run share|share_pct`, `TYPICAL_PEAK|18-20|peak at`, `0.7`, `tier note|25% below`, `base floor|25 mi|MARATHON_BASE|Higdon|Pfitz`, `newer|low.volume tier|level 1`, `65 lb|seven to nine|three hours of running|100 minutes|WEEKLY_HOUR_OPTIONS`. Code-side: `grep -n accessoryBands|capDisplay|capMiles|paceMinPerMile|easyPaceMinPerMile|hoursForTier|COMMITMENT_TIERS|floorForGoal c/NonRaceBuilder.tsx`; `grep -rn "1.25|0.75|bodyweight" sf/materialize-plan/index.ts`; `grep -rn "48.*hour|48h" sf/generate-combined-plan/*.ts`; `grep -rln` of each `src/lib` entry file across `src` and `supabase/functions` for importers; `grep -n "CLIMB_.*_MK" sf/_shared/group-ride-route-snapshot.ts`.

**Steps on no route (dead code, numbers computed on the phone, never shown today)** — `lib/wizard-steps.ts:272-382` never pushes them: `commitment` "≈ 6 / 9 / 12 h/wk" (`lib/non-race-goal-seeds.ts:88-98`, no source); `length` "At least N weeks … N–52" (`floorForGoal`, `c/NonRaceBuilder.tsx:4803-4822`); `run` "This block holds about 13 mi — two-thirds of normal" (`maintenanceDoseFor` = `round(usual × 2/3)`, Hickson cited in `lib/maintenance-volume-band.ts:67-74`), "Not sure" → `startLightMiles()` = `round(60 ÷ 10)` = 6 (:26-34,54-57), band lines at 60–180 min/wk and ±25% (:27-31,75-90, "Ours" in comment :76), placeholder `capDisplay − 4` from `capMiles = round(180 ÷ paceMinPerMile)` with `paceMinPerMile = easy sec/km × 1.609344 ÷ 60 else 10` (`c/NonRaceBuilder.tsx:1953-1956,7024` — computed on every render; the `easyPaceMinPerMile` argument to `assemblePayload` :1236 is accepted and never read); `bike` "About 1h20 a ride" = `hours ÷ rideDays` (:7120-7128); `volume` "Over 8 hours a week of endurance, accessory lifting drops to the minimum." (:6257-6260) mirroring `resolveEnduranceTier` `> 8 h or ≥ 2 hard days` (`lib/assistance-menu.ts:194-220`) — `accessoryBands` (:3101-3129) is computed and rendered nowhere (grep `accessoryBands` → one hit); non-strength `accessory` pull-up row "tracked to the 50-in-10 session standard", "Untested, so this starts band-assisted at 50 reps a week" (`lib/pullup-progression.ts:47-48,218-300`: 100 ÷ 2 rounded to 5, scaled `100 × cap ÷ FULL_DOSE_CAPACITY`; the 100/week and 50-in-10 are attributed in comments to a retired programme, no ledger row); `swim` 1/2/3; `hardday` (deleted body).

**Flags, grouped**

TWO
- "Start the week of" default Monday: phone next-Monday local (`c/NonRaceBuilder.tsx:1215-1224`), season wizard next-Monday UTC with Monday → +7 (`c/ArcSetupWizard.tsx:335-341`), server Monday-of-week UTC (`sf/create-goal-and-materialize-plan/index.ts:480-486`).
- Tier numbers "6 miles", "40+ miles" typed in `FITNESS_TIERS` (`c/NonRaceBuilder.tsx:494-496`) and held again in `TIER_SEEDS` (`lib/run-volume-tables.ts:703-707`).

### Flags, grouped

**TWO**
- Lift header length: plan screen hands the Planned tab a raw `planned_workouts` row (no `planned_duration_label`) so a lift prints the composer's constant "55:00" (`c/PlannedSessionHeader.tsx:118-125`; `sf/_shared/standing-plan/compose.ts:3124`) while Today prints "30–40 min" (`sf/get-week/planned-duration-label.ts:33-49`).
- Same screen, two lengths: header `mm:00` rounded vs "Total duration: m:ss" exact (`c/PlannedSessionHeader.tsx:103` vs `c/StructuredPlannedView.tsx:615-617`).
- First open of a plan shows `sessions_by_week` (authored minutes, phone-substituted paces) and overwrites the materialized week; week change reloads rows (`c/AllPlansInterface.tsx:486,492-660,977-1000`) — inferred.
- Pace range tolerance ±2%/±6% (`sf/materialize-plan/index.ts:3781-3793`) vs ±5% (`sf/get-week/index.ts:748-766`); FTP-% parser duplicated (`materialize-plan:3523-3553` vs `get-week:770-792`).
- Race-day single pace decided by tag on the phone and by a zero-width range on the server (`c/PlannedWorkoutSummary.tsx:372-378`; `materialize-plan:3781`).
- Description miles prefix rewritten on the phone from step metres (`c/StructuredPlannedView.tsx:382-430`) vs the server's text.
- Swim display unit: four-rung phone chain (`c/StructuredPlannedView.tsx:159-171`) vs server two-rung (`sf/_shared/swim/swim-plan-summary.ts:144`).
- Three strength-line composers: server `display_line` (`sf/_shared/strength/strength-display-lines.ts:35-88`), drawer `plainLiftList` (`src/utils/strengthFormatter.ts:82-122`), export (`c/AllPlansInterface.tsx:1452-1512`).
- Custom session length: phone saves Σ duration × repeatCount as `duration` (`c/WorkoutBuilder.tsx:203,390`); server ladder reads `intervals[]` first and does not multiply (`sf/_shared/planned-duration.ts:98-112,135-142`).
- Plan-overview week minutes: blob weeks = authored `duration`, materialized weeks = stored total (`sf/_shared/plan-overview.ts:174-180`).
- Week index: get-week `weekNumberFor` from the raw start (`sf/get-week/index.ts:148-155`) vs plan-overview snapped to week-start (`sf/_shared/plan-week.ts:44-63`) — inferred which surfaces the former reaches.
- "weeks to go" = ceil (`c/AllPlansInterface.tsx:1691`) vs State's round (TRUTH-MAP §6, `_shared/goal-context.ts:88`).
- "caps at 100 minutes" typed in copy (`lib/standing-plan-week-copy.ts:1557`) and as a constant (`sf/_shared/standing-plan/volume-bounds.ts:111`).
- Schedule gate: ride needs ≥2 days at `lib/schedule-gate.ts:99`, message fires at <1 at `:144`.

**PHONE**
- `mm:00` rounding of planned seconds everywhere the phone prints a length (`c/PlannedSessionHeader.tsx:90-104`; `c/PlannedWorkoutSummary.tsx:6-10`; `c/AllPlansInterface.tsx:442,994`; `c/StructuredPlannedView.tsx:381`).

**Flags, grouped**

*TWO:*
- Finish time formatting: three phone formatters for one `goals.current_value` — `c/AthleticRecordPage.tsx:33` (always seconds), `c/GoalsScreen.tsx:182-190` (drops `:00`), `c/context/state-primitives.tsx:77`.
- Typed race time parsers disagree on "4:38": `c/AthleticRecordPage.tsx:41-49` (4 min 38 s), `c/GoalsScreen.tsx:192-201` (4 h 38 m when first part ≤ 12), `src/lib/effort-score.ts:26-46` (by distance).
- Finish-seconds ladder duplicated: `sf/_shared/race-finish-seconds.ts:21-35` and `src/lib/race-finish-seconds.ts:19-33` (phone copy, no importer).
- "N weeks away" (`c/GoalsScreen.tsx:1362`, truncate) vs "Nw out" (`sf/_shared/goal-context.ts:89`, round) vs projection weeks (`sf/_shared/recompute-goal-race-projections.ts:22`, ceil).
- Course pacing gate on the phone (`src/lib/goal-target-time.ts:5-17`, `c/GoalsScreen.tsx:1312-1322`) vs server anchor chain (`sf/_shared/resolve-server-predicted-finish.ts:124-158`).
- Course sheet header anchor (`sf/course-detail/index.ts:178-260`) vs the anchor the groups were built from (`sf/course-strategy/index.ts:192-245`).
- Terrain-adjusted finish loop in `sf/course-detail/index.ts:350-369` and `sf/course-strategy/index.ts:377-391` (15-s gate only in the second).
- "Your goal": State prefers `plans.config.target_time` (`c/context/StateRaceBlock.tsx:91-105`); My Record and `sf/_shared/resolve-goal-target-time.ts:24-52` prefer `goals.target_time`.
- Official result: phone goals query `c/context/StateTab.tsx:199-221` vs coach `last_completed_race` `sf/coach/index.ts:3240-3282`.
- "Model had projected": live `race_readiness` (`StateTab.tsx:326-333`) vs stored `race_result.projected_seconds` (`sf/complete-race/index.ts:166-203`, `sf/coach/index.ts:3268-3279`, `sf/course-detail/index.ts:200-210`), which snapshots a different field (`fitness_projection_seconds`).
- Three stored copies of the projection: `goals.projection` (`recompute-goal-race-projections.ts:183-196`), `goals.race_readiness_projection` (`sf/coach/index.ts:3432-3441`), `coach_cache.race_finish_projection_v1`.

- Assessment bands copied: `race-readiness/index.ts:283-288` and `arc-context.ts:444-450` (§6).

### Flags, grouped

**TWO**
- `swim_as_planned` written by the popup (`c/PostWorkoutFeedback.tsx:413`) vs `swam_as_planned` read by `sf/compute-facts/index.ts:1356`, `sf/compute-snapshot/index.ts:1099`, `src/hooks/useSwimBaselineNudge.ts:37` (only `sf/learn-fitness-profile/index.ts:405` reads the written name).
- Share text length reads `workout.moving_seconds` off the drawer prop (`lib/share-session-text.ts:31`, `c/UnifiedWorkoutView.tsx:1163`); after `c/AppLayout.tsx:770-778` refreshes the row with `select('*')` there is no such field (not a column); the drawer header reads `session_detail_v1.completed_totals`.
- Distance conversion: popup `workouts.distance ÷ 1.60934` (`c/PostWorkoutFeedback.tsx:454`), share `overall.distance_m ÷ 1609.34` (`lib/share-session-text.ts:45`), export `÷ 1.609344` (`sf/export-data:35`).
- Share pace/speed/HR rebuilt on the phone (`lib/share-session-text.ts:49-58`) and duplicated in `c/TodaysEffort.tsx:594-605`; Details/Perf read stored fields (TRUTH-MAP §6).
- "lb moved" = added weight × reps (`sf/_shared/strava/strength-description.ts:62-74`) vs Performance/Today volume (bodyweight + added) × reps for chin-up/dip/pull-up (`sf/_shared/workload.ts:436`, `get-week:952`).
- Strava `elapsed_time` ladder (`sf/share-strength-to-strava/index.ts:88-91`) vs `completedMovingSeconds` for the same session in the share text.
- Export `profile.json.weight_unit` "kg" on a metric account (`sf/export-data/index.ts:279`) vs sets written `unit: 'lb'` (`c/StrengthLogger.tsx:2182,3968`).
- Export `duration_min` = raw `workouts.duration` minutes (`sf/export-data:200`) vs the moving ladder on every screen (inferred).
- Effort number stored as `workouts.rpe` (popup, `c/PostWorkoutFeedback.tsx:376`) vs `workout_metadata.session_rpe` (logger, `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:3977`); checkpoint reads `session_rpe ?? rpe` (`sf/endurance-checkpoint/index.ts:188`).
- Soreness stored in `workout_metadata.readiness.soreness` (popup) vs `readiness_checkins.soreness` read by the soreness signal (`sf/_shared/longitudinal-signals.ts:611-616`).
- FTP proposal rule runs on the phone (`c/PostWorkoutFeedback.tsx:126-133`) and on the server for the checkpoint (`sf/endurance-checkpoint/index.ts:140-144`).
- Accept writers: popup/Adjust through `save-baselines` (`lib/accept-measured.ts:5-8`); checkpoint writes `user_baselines.learned_fitness` directly (`sf/endurance-checkpoint/index.ts:214-246`).
- Garmin percent-only ride steps price off the live FTP (`sf/_shared/garmin/prepare.ts:10-11`); the row's watts and Intervals.icu percentages use the frozen `computed.anchors.ftp_w` (`sf/_shared/intervals/serialize.ts:96`).
- Garmin `paceTarget` text km → mi uses 1.60934 (`convert-workout.ts:155`); everywhere else 1.609344 / 1609.34.
- "Longest ride" compares the moving ladder (this session) with stored `overall.duration_s_moving` (priors), `sf/_shared/session-boom/line.ts:297-313` (inferred).
- Popup writes `number_of_active_lengths` from its own division (`c/PostWorkoutFeedback.tsx:392`) over the analyser's count (TRUTH-MAP §6 swim card).

**PHONE**
- Popup: date word (`:481-500`), distance unit + constant (`:449-459`), FTP/threshold proposal rule + rounding (`:126-135`), sec/km → sec/mi and `Math.round` remainder (`:125,134,145`), pool prefill 0.6 m match (`:265,283`), length count (`:392`).
- Checkpoint evidence pace: `workouts.avg_pace` is sec/km (`sf/ingest-activity:511`), the sheet prints it as `/mi` (`c/context/EnduranceCheckpointSheet.tsx:27`).
- Share text: every number rebuilt from row fields (`lib/share-session-text.ts:35-61`); moving_seconds dependent on which path loaded the drawer.
- Swim nudge: whole rule + 70-day fetch + rounding (`lib/swimBaselineNudge.ts`, `src/hooks/useSwimBaselineNudge.ts:19`, `c/context/StateSwimNudge.tsx:23`).
- Signal choice: `lib/nudge-policy.ts:56-93` (concern, else two warnings in one category); `info` signals on the allow-list (`strength_rir_above_prescription`, `chronic_short_sleep`, and easy-pace under 20 s/mi) never render.
- Snooze 7 d in `localStorage` (`c/context/state-primitives.tsx:14-24`).
- Weekly-limit toast counts the week's rows on the phone (`c/AllPlansInterface.tsx:714-726`).
- Advance line rule (`lib/advance-nudge.ts:64-133`).
- Strength finish-sheet default 5 when the effort is cleared (`c/StrengthLogger.tsx:6860`).
- Plan markdown brick hours re-derived from the name or minutes (`c/AllPlansInterface.tsx:1370-1395`).

**NO-SRC**
- Swim effort words (`c/ui/effort-scale.tsx:20-23`); strength default 5 (`c/StrengthLogger.tsx:6860`); "exactly one planned run that day" talk-test rule (`sf/check-feedback-needed:98-100`); feeling words (`c/PostWorkoutFeedback.tsx:76-82`); pool 0.6 m match (`:265,283`); equipment pace sentence (`:820`).
- Good-news pace-per-100-bpm normalisation (`sf/_shared/session-boom/line.ts:172-212`; no ledger row).
- Weekly quality cap 1 (`optional-ui-spec.json:6`); swim nudge 4 wk / 4 swims / 10 d / 70 d; snooze 7 d.

## 8. Stage 1 verdicts — every flag in §6 and §7 re-opened (2026-09-15)

> **Read-only verification, code as of `9b1c9171`.** Ten passes, one per section of §6 and §7. Every flagged `file:line` was re-read with ten lines either side; every TWO flag had both places opened; every NO-SRC constant was grepped in its comments, `STATE-SOURCES.md` and `DECISIONS-LOG.md`. **Verdicts:** CONFIRMED · CORRECTED (line, wording or rank changed) · STRUCK (formatting only, dead code, citation found, or not reproducible) · UNVERIFIED. **Rank:** A = a wrong or contradicting number an athlete can see today · B = right today but two places can drift · C = missing citation or ledger row only · D = formatting, dead code, cosmetic. Totals: **confirmed 308 · corrected 88 · struck 85 · unverified 4** over 481 flag lines. **Rank A: 42 distinct items after de-duplication across sections (§8.0).** Nothing was changed.

### 8.0 Rank A — the numbers an athlete can see wrong today, de-duplicated across sections

**Unit and label errors (fire on every account)**
1. Checkpoint sheet evidence line prints hard-run pace in sec/km through the per-mile formatter — about 38 % too fast (`sf/endurance-checkpoint/index.ts:189`; `c/context/EnduranceCheckpointSheet.tsx:27`). [§7.5 #9, §7.7 row 30a]
2. "7:60/mi" is printable: remainder rounded separately from minutes in the popup threshold card (`c/PostWorkoutFeedback.tsx:113`), share text (`lib/share-session-text.ts:51`), Today card (`c/TodaysEffort.tsx:598`). [§7.7 row 5]
3. Interval table prints raw pace under the "GAP" header on rows under 400 m (`c/EnduranceIntervalTable.tsx:298,314`); ledger row 151 says "—". [§6 Perf A3]
4. Baselines "from your lifts, N sets" / "three logged sets" — N counts sessions (`c/TrainingBaselines.tsx:1330,1339`; `sf/compute-facts:1795-1816`). [§7.5 #2]
5. Baselines threshold note "from runs, 5 runs" — N is fitted distance points (`c/TrainingBaselines.tsx:1218`; `sf/learn-fitness-profile:962`). [§7.5 #7]
6. Map overlay pace on a run prints "--": the server writes no speed line for runs (`sf/compute-workout-analysis/display-series.ts:251`; `c/EffortsViewerMapbox.tsx:507,716`). [§6 Details]

**Metric-account errors (metric is selectable at signup, `c/WelcomePage.tsx:389`; no metric account exists in prod)**
7. Lifts printed "kg" on Adjust/Baselines, "lb" in the logger, priced as lb everywhere; a typed kg is saved raw as lb (`c/context/StateAdjustLens.tsx:123,295`; `c/TrainingBaselines.tsx:1329`; `c/StrengthLogger.tsx:6168`). **Two screens, two numbers for one fact (every account)**

**Rank A list**
- A1 Ride efficiency factor: Performance divides NP by pedalling-only HR (`sf/_shared/cycling-v1/ride-physiology.ts:132-137`), State divides NP by the whole-ride average HR (`sf/compute-facts/index.ts:1255,1263`); same ride, two EFs; the ledger (`STATE-SOURCES:15`) backs State's.
- A2 Drift on a long session with sets: Performance prints the windowed pct (`sf/_shared/session-detail/build.ts:882-907`), Today's streak line uses the whole-file pct (`sf/_shared/session-boom/line.ts:165,359-366`); under-5 on one screen, streak broken on the other.
- A3 Interval table GAP column: a row under 400 m prints its raw pace under the "GAP" header (`c/EnduranceIntervalTable.tsx:298,314`); `STATE-SOURCES:151` says "—".
- A4 Swim pace label: metric plan + imperial athlete → seconds per 100 m printed as "/100yd" on Performance (`build.ts:753,763`; `c/EnduranceIntervalTable.tsx:500,539`); Details shows the right unit (`c/CompletedTab.tsx:1290-1294`), so the two tabs disagree.
- A5 Strength estimate on an RIR-tracked protocol: Performance "Estimated max" uses raw reps (`sf/_shared/strength/all-out-set.ts:149`), facts/State e1RM uses reps + average RIR (`sf/compute-facts/strength-facts-lib.ts:200-209`); same set, two numbers.

**Rank A list**
1. Readiness "Why" on State can print "load elevated (ACWR 1.xx)" while the ledger says ACWR is off every surface — `sf/coach/index.ts:4620-4622` → `c/context/StateHeaderBlock.tsx:86` (fatigued/overreached, no loaded-legs line, ACWR ≥ 1.2).
2. READINESS "Nd ago" uses a UTC date — a check-in logged today reads "yesterday" after UTC midnight (5 pm PDT) — `c/context/StateReadinessRow.tsx:17-21`.
3. Two easy paces: State row = median of the classified easy group's last five (`run.ts:151-163`); Adjust = learner's last five else threshold × 1.19 (`resolve-current-run-pace.ts:181-187`); ledger row 88 admits they differ by seconds.
4. Two FTPs for an athlete on manual with a learned estimate: State bike anchor = accepted/estimate, never manual (`compute-snapshot:1940`, `resolve-current-ftp.ts:196-201`); Adjust = manual wins (`StateAdjustLens.tsx:125`, resolver tier 0 `:141-144`).
5. Adjust lift numbers for a metric athlete: a pound value printed under "kg" (`StateAdjustLens.tsx:123`; `capacity-resolver.ts:79`), and a typed kg saved raw as lb (`:295`).
6. Completed-race card "Model had projected" is today's live projection, not the stored pre-race one — `c/context/StateTab.tsx:321-337` vs `sf/coach/index.ts:3268-3279`.

**Counts:** confirmed 37 · corrected 13 · struck 5 · unverified 1 (56 flag lines across 30 rows)

Rank A list
- ⓘ form key on Today's load card (and State's ⓘ, same component): "Today: 47 − 63 = −15" — the two operands are rounded separately, the result is the server's form rounded, so the printed subtraction can be off by 1 (`c/LoadBar.tsx:91,130-131`).
- Reschedule popup "Workload impact — Daily 45 → 130": a second workload formula (`sf/validate-reschedule/index.ts:192-221`) prices the same session differently from the drawer's "Workload 62" chip (`workload_planned`, `sf/_shared/workload.ts`); both are on screen for one session.
- Reschedule popup "Weekly 310 → 395": the "before" weekly total is summed over the NEW date's ±3-day window (`:557-565,593-601`), so it drops the old window's days that fall outside it — an under-counted before-number beside a full after-number.

> Route facts this section rests on (I read): `wizard-steps.ts:332` race = goal→race→level→intent→days→strength→numbers→confirm; `:368-371` strength head adds `posture` only when `fixedSportScope` is null, and `:264-270` returns non-null for every `get_stronger`; `:118-184` `scheduleSteps` pushes `run`/`bike` only for a goal that is neither strength nor race, `swim` only on race; `:376-381` numbers before schedule on the strength path. Goal is only ever `get_stronger` or `marathon` (greps above), so `posture`, `commitment`, `length`, `run`, `bike`, `volume`, `swim`, `hardday` and the non-strength `accessory` screens are on no route. Season wizard `ArcSetupWizard` is live via `/arc-setup` (three entry points above).

**Rank A list**
1. Race step "About N weeks of training" uses the intermediate floor before the level is tapped (`NRB:1365`, `CG:3735`): a first-timer 12 weeks out reads 12 on the race step, then 14 on the level step and the confirm; an advanced runner 9 weeks out reads 10, then 9.
2. Metric athlete on the level step: the tier seed lands in the km field unconverted (`NRB:4388`), is sent as 19 mi (`:3763`), and the same screen prints "You have entered 19 miles a week. This tier assumes about 30." (`rvt:732`, `NRB:4469-4471`); the plan builds off 19, not 30. Fires on all three tiers.
3. Season wizard "Week 1 starts" default (`ASW:335-341,358`): after 17:00 Pacific the field shows a Tuesday (local next-Monday date sliced in UTC); on a Monday it skips a week; the server (`CG:480-486`) silently starts the plan on that week's Monday, up to six days before the date shown.
4. Season wizard run-pace note and confirm line print "/mi" for a metric athlete (`ASW:114-119,1377-1391,2205`) while the numbers step prints the same pace "/km" (`KYN:66-68`).
5. Season wizard confirm "· N weeks to race" rounds up (`weeks-until-race.ts:11`) while Today's arc line rounds to nearest (`build-arc-line.ts:58`): a race 45 days out reads 7 on the confirm and 6 on Today.

**Flags, grouped — verdicts** (each maps to the row above; nothing new unless stated)

TWO: one lift three numbers → 1b (B) · learned lift rounding → 3b (STRUCK D) · threshold HR inputs → 8b (**A**) · easy pace → 10b (**A**) · FTP → 16b (**A**) · max HR → 30 (**A**) · test scheduling → 14/19 (CORRECTED: two events + dead pickers, C) · deload ‖ 12 → 21 (B) · age → 23 (B) · lift save shape → 38 (B) · pace suffix → 42 (B).

PHONE: every resolver on the phone → 1, 6, 8, 10, 16 (B: the same `src/lib` pure functions the server imports — `easy-hr.ts:50-52`, `zones.ts:52`, `endurance-checkpoint/index.ts:127-129`; the phone re-runs them, it does not re-decide them); unit picks with no conversion → 2 (**A**), 24/25 (B); pace km↔mi → 6 (D), 41 (B); Z2 note → 13 (STRUCK D); deload +1 → 21 (B); test dates → 14/19 (C); labels by rule → 4, 26, 31, 32, 36 (STRUCK D), 27 (**A**), 28 (B), 34 "–null" (CORRECTED D), 37 (B), 22 WATCH (B); max HR chain → 30 (**A**); resting → 33 (C); "Last updated" → 26 (STRUCK D); checkpoint "/mi" → 39 (**A**), evidence sec/km → 40 (**A**).

NO-SRC: learned lift 84 d / 6–3 → 3 (C) · threshold HR fill-ins → 9 (C) · easy filters 20 min / 150–900 → 11 (C, D-284 names the 20 min) · easy band 70 % / 65–80 % → 12 (CORRECTED: 80 % has field receipts in D-282, 65 % and 70 % do not; C) · ratchet + 90 d → 17, 20 (C) · retest 3 d / 2 d + "within a day" → 14, 15, 19, 20 (C) · VDOT → 28, 29 (C) · Tanaka / Gulati / Karvonen / Z4-Z5 → 30, 34 (CORRECTED: Tanaka cited at DECISIONS-LOG:2864; the rest uncited; C) · resting > 30 → 33 (C) · swim copy → 35 (C) · checkpoint week 6 / large move / split → CORRECTED: `CHECKPOINT_IS_OURS` (`endurance-checkpoint.ts:47-49`) and the book text exists at SOURCE-viada:376-378 ("after six weeks", "several seconds per kilometer"); no STATE-SOURCES row (grep `checkpoint\|week 6\|six weeks` → rows 58-59 only) (C).

LEDGER≠CODE: row 85 "48 hours" → CONFIRMED (ledger stale): code `baseline-test-rows.ts:34-36, 106-109` says the sentence was never on the page; SOURCE-viada grep "48 hours" → 0 (C) · row 126 sessions vs sets → 5 (CORRECTED: ledger right, screen wrong; **A**) · row 45 `manual_ride_lthr` → CONFIRMED: `TrainingBaselines.tsx:1167` writes `{ rideLthr: v }` with no source flag; grep `manual_ride_lthr` in STATE-SOURCES → 0 (C).

**Rank A list**
1. Lift unit on a metric account: Adjust and Baselines print the lift as "kg" (`StateAdjustLens.tsx:123`, `TrainingBaselines.tsx:1329`), the logger prints the same number as "lb" (`StrengthLogger.tsx:6168, 6185`), and every consumer rounds and prices it as pounds (`estimate-1rm.ts:170-173`, `workload.ts:303-310`, `working-number.ts:240`); no converter exists for lifts (grep).
2. Baselines strength note and info: "from your lifts, N sets" / "three logged sets and up" (`TrainingBaselines.tsx:1330, 1339`) — N counts sessions (one `exercise_log` row per exercise per workout, `compute-facts:1795-1816`; ledger row 126 says sessions).
3. Threshold heart rate, run: Adjust can show the bike's typed threshold HR as the run number through `cfg.threshold_heart_rate` (`resolve-current-lthr.ts:187`, written as run ‖ ride by `derive.ts:249`); Baselines' run row cannot see that field (`TrainingBaselines.tsx:1146`) — two screens, two numbers for an athlete with only a bike threshold typed.
4. Easy pace: Adjust/Baselines print the learner's last-five median (`learn-fitness-profile:876-880`); State prints its own last-five median over a differently chosen group (ledger row 88 records that they differ).
5. FTP with "your number" set: State's bike row shows the learned FTP (`compute-snapshot:1940` `appliedLearnedFtp`, no manual tier); Adjust, Baselines, the zones and the plan use the typed FTP (`resolve-current-ftp.ts:133-136`).
6. Max heart rate on Baselines: an age estimate is shown as the max ("188 bpm · auto", `TrainingBaselines.tsx:1157, 1163`) while the server's zone build refuses it (`derive.ts:232-233 allowAgeEstimate:false`) and the same section then says zones need a max (`:1194`).
7. Threshold pace note: "from runs, 5 runs" (`TrainingBaselines.tsx:1218`) prints the number of fitted distance points (`learn-fitness-profile:962 sample_count: fit.nPoints`), not runs.
8. Checkpoint sheet paces: always "/mi" (`EnduranceCheckpointSheet.tsx:16`) on a kg · km account.
9. Checkpoint evidence line: hard-run pace is `workouts.avg_pace` in seconds per km (`endurance-checkpoint/index.ts:189`; `arc-context.ts:586`) printed through the per-mile formatter (`EnduranceCheckpointSheet.tsx:27`) — a 5:00/km run reads "5:00/mi" for every athlete.

**Counts (47 table rows incl. the b-rows; grouped items map onto them):** confirmed 29 · corrected 9 · struck 9 · unverified 0. Rank A 9 · B 15 · C 13 · D 10 (rows with two ranks counted at the higher).

**Rank A list**
- #2 · The same typed time means different things on two screens: "4:44" on My Record's Add race saves 4 min 44 s; on the Goals "Add elapsed result" prompt it saves 4 h 44 min. Both write the same race row (`AthleticRecordPage.tsx:46,186-188`; `GoalsScreen.tsx:197-198,1456,1464-1466`).
- #4 / #17 · Weeks to the race: Goals truncates, the State header rounds — at 11 days out Goals says "1 week away" and State "2w out"; a past-dated goal with no result shows a negative "weeks away" (`GoalsScreen.tsx:1354-1362`; `goal-context.ts:89`).
- #6 · Course sheet header and its segment paces can come from different anchors: header "Your goal 3:45:00" (plan target) while the segment paces sum to the fitness projection (`course-detail:214-224`; `course-strategy:222-235`); for a finished race the header shows the pre-race projection and the segments the actual finish.
- #10 · "Model had projected … vs model" under a finished race can print the NEXT race's projection: State takes the live readiness number with no goal check, and readiness moves to the next future run goal after race day (`StateTab.tsx:325-333`; `goal-context.ts:101-106`; `coach:3143-3153`).
- #15 · My Record "Add race" with no linked goal inserts `completed_at`, a column `complete-race:215-221` says the live database does not have, with no retry (`AthleticRecordPage.tsx:199-212`) — if that comment still holds, every such save fails. Live schema not checked this session.
